## Über mich sprechen (Talking about me)

`de-DE.fl.en-about-me` teaches `fl.grammar.chunks` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Fixed sentence patterns for talking about yourself: I am …, I have got …, I can …, I like …. Lesson 1 hears and matches I am, I have got and I can with pictures and numbers (I am eight. I have got a dog.); lesson 2 adds I like, chooses the right pattern for what you want to say and puts the words of a sentence in order, and ends with the skill check. Instructions are in German; the sentences are spoken in English and shown in short form.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. I am, I have got, I can | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Ein Satz über mich | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: audio_choice 18, multiple_choice 13, matching 1, true_false 2, ordering 4, fill_in_blank 4. Builds on: `fl.themes.numbers-to-10`, `fl.listening.familiar-words`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

`de-DE.fl.en-animals` teaches `fl.themes.outside-animals` in de-DE, stufe-0. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Animals and things outside in English, and what an animal can do (A bird can fly.). Stufe 0 is oral only: instructions are spoken in German, the words in English. ## Fragen stellen und beantworten (Asking and answering)

`de-DE.fl.en-ask-answer` teaches `fl.speaking.ask-answer` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Asking simple questions and giving the answer that fits. Lesson 1 asks Have you got ...?, What's this? and What colour is it? and answers them (Yes, I have. It's a book. It's green.); lesson 2 asks Where is ...? and How many ...?, matches each question with the kind of answer it wants (a place, a number), and ends with the skill check. Speaking tasks are practice only.

## Tage, Monate, Geburtstag (Days, months and birthdays)

`de-DE.fl.en-calendar` teaches `fl.themes.calendar` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 brings the days of the week, in order, and "What day is it today? It's Monday.", with the weekend and the days that are easy to mix up (Tuesday and Thursday). Lesson 2 brings the twelve months, the four seasons and "When's your birthday? It's in May.", and ends with the skill check. Instructions are in German; the English is spoken and shown, and a table shows the days and months in order.

## Farben auf Englisch (Colours in English)

`de-DE.fl.en-colours` teaches `fl.themes.colours` in de-DE, stufe-0. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 2.

Eight colours, heard and said before they are ever read. Stufe 0 is oral only: every task can be done without reading. Instructions are spoken in German, the colour words in English. Lesson 1 brings red, blue, yellow and green; lesson 2 adds orange, purple, pink and brown and ends with the skill check. Practice runs in three stages: full hints, one hint, no hints, and the last stage puts the colours on things. Each lesson also pairs colours with things that have them (matching) and sorts pictures by colour (sorting); tapping a colour says its English name.

## Englisch und Deutsch vergleichen (Comparing English and German)

`de-DE.fl.en-compare-languages` teaches `fl.culture-strategies.compare-languages` in de-DE, stufe-0. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children compare English words with German ones. Lesson 1 finds words that sound almost the same (Haus, house; Milch, milk; Wasser, water; Buch, book) and sets them against words that sound quite different (Hund, dog). Lesson 2 meets false friends a child might trip over: the Rock is a skirt in English (rock is a stone), the Hose is trousers, the Handy is a mobile phone and a Kind is a child (kind means nett). It mixes them with true pairs and ends with the skill check. Oral only: nothing is read.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Haus und house | yes | 18 | learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Falsche Freunde | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: true_false 21, image_multiple_choice 4, audio_choice 9, speak 6. Builds on: . Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Sätze nach Muster schreiben (Writing sentences from a model)

`de-DE.fl.en-complete-sentences` teaches `fl.writing.complete-sentences` in de-DE, lernjahr-2. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Completing English sentences from a model and building new ones to the same pattern: I like ..., I have got a ..., I can ..., I am ... . Lesson 1 fills the gap in a model sentence from a picture and learns which pattern says what; lesson 2 changes one part of a model to make a new sentence of the child's own, with a capital I, a full stop and a or an, and ends with the skill check. The child writes by choosing word cards and putting them in order; free writing is not marked.

## Wörter abschreiben (Copying words)

`de-DE.fl.en-copy-words` teaches `fl.writing.copy-words` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Copying familiar English words exactly and labelling pictures. Lesson 1 copies short words on writing lines, lays them with letter cards, fills missing letters from a model and spots copying mistakes; lesson 2 labels pictures for a picture dictionary with longer words and double letters (rabbit, yellow, giraffe) and ends with the skill check. The model word is always on screen. Writing is traced with a finger; the app accepts any ink for now, so the skill check uses the card and choice tasks only.

## Alltag in England und bei uns (Daily life in England and here)

`de-DE.fl.en-daily-life` teaches `fl.culture-strategies.compare-daily-life` in de-DE, lernjahr-2. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Comparing a child's day in England with a day here, through Amy, who is nine and lives in England. Lesson 1 is about school: the school uniform, a school day from nine o'clock to half past three, lunch at school (school dinner or packed lunch), and starting school at four or five, so that Amy is in Year 5. Lesson 2 is about breakfast (cereal, toast, the big breakfast at the weekend) and celebrations (Bonfire Night here and not St Martin), and ends with the skill check. Every lesson names what is the same as well as what differs, and says "many children", not "all children".


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Ein Schultag in England | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Frühstück und Feste | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: audio_choice 13, multiple_choice 15, true_false 7, sorting 5, fill_in_blank 4, matching 2. Builds on: `fl.culture-strategies.where-spoken`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Hallo, wie geht's? (Hello, how are you?)

`de-DE.fl.en-greet` teaches `fl.speaking.greet` in de-DE, stufe-0. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children greet someone and keep the conversation going. Lesson 1 asks How are you? and answers I'm fine, thank you, I'm OK or I'm sad, then asks back with And you? Lesson 2 adds I'm Mia for the name, How old are you? and I'm seven, puts a whole greeting together from hello to goodbye, and ends with the skill check. Hello, goodbye, please and thank you come from the Hello lessons and are used here in short exchanges. Speaking is practised and not marked; the checks ask whether a reply fits. Oral only: nothing is read.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. How are you? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. I'm Mia. I'm seven. | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: speak 13, audio_choice 11, true_false 18. Builds on: `fl.speaking.imitate`. Misconceptions addressed: 4.

## Neue Wörter erraten (Guessing new words)

`de-DE.fl.en-guess-meaning` teaches `fl.culture-strategies.guess-meaning` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Working out what an unknown English word means instead of giving up. Lesson 1 brings two tricks: look at the picture (hedgehog, snail, octopus) and listen for a German word that sounds alike (garden, summer, milk), with a warning that a word can sound alike and still mean something else (butterfly). Lesson 2 adds two more: the words you know in the sentence (thirsty next to water) and what the speaker does (a wave, a finger on the lips), and ends with the skill check. The English is spoken; questions and answers are in German.

## Gut zuhören, genau nachsprechen (Listen and say it like me)

`de-DE.fl.en-imitate` teaches `fl.speaking.imitate` in de-DE, stufe-0. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children hear an English word or a short sentence and say it back, with attention to three sounds German does not have in the same way. Lesson 1 is th: the tongue sits between the teeth (three, thank you, mouth), heard against the German-sounding t and s (tree, mouse). Lesson 2 is w with round lips (whale, water, web), heard against v (van), and the soft English r (red, rabbit, rose); it ends with the skill check. Speaking is practised, not marked: the skill check asks the child to hear the sounds apart, which comes before saying them well. Oral only: nothing is read.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Three, thank you | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Whale, water, red | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: audio_choice 21, speak 13, true_false 8. Builds on: `fl.listening.familiar-words`. Misconceptions addressed: 4.

## Berufe auf Englisch (Jobs in English)

`de-DE.fl.en-jobs` teaches `fl.themes.jobs` in de-DE, lernjahr-2. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Naming jobs and saying what someone does at work. Lesson 1 brings doctor, teacher, farmer, cook, police officer and firefighter with "He's a …" and "She's a …" (the English job word is the same for women and men); lesson 2 says what each one does (A cook cooks food.) and asks "What do you want to be?", with "I want", not "I will", for ich will, and ends with the skill check. Instructions are in German; the English is spoken and shown in short form.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. He's a doctor. | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. What do you want to be? | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 7, audio_choice 18, speak 6, true_false 4, matching 3, ordering 4. Builds on: `fl.grammar.he-she-it`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Wörter lernen und behalten (Learning and remembering words)

`de-DE.fl.en-learn-words` teaches `fl.culture-strategies.learn-words` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Ways to remember new English words. Lesson 1 brings picture cards (picture on the front, word on the back), saying the word aloud with a movement (jump, clap, wave), and a few words each day instead of many at once. Lesson 2 brings the word notebook (English on the left, German on the right), sorting words by topic, and finding words in a picture dictionary by topic and by ABC, and ends with the skill check. Instructions are in German; the English words are read and heard.

Saying what you like and do not like, and asking others. ## Für Oma auf Deutsch sagen (Telling Grandma in German)

`de-DE.fl.en-mediate` teaches `fl.culture-strategies.mediate` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Sprachmittlung: telling someone in German what a short English sign or sentence means. The Becker family is on holiday in London and Grandma speaks no English. Lesson 1 explains signs (OPEN, CLOSED, PUSH, PULL, TOILETS, NO DOGS, WAY OUT) by saying what they mean rather than word for word. Lesson 2 passes on short spoken and written sentences (a waiter, a bus driver, a card from a friend), turning "I" into the person who said it, and ends with the skill check. The English is read or heard; the answers are in German.

`de-DE.fl.en-role-play` teaches `fl.speaking.role-play` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Two short everyday scenes with fixed phrases. Lesson 1 is in a shop: Finn buys fruit from Mrs Hill (Can I help you? Can I have …, please? Here you are. How much is it? One pound, please.). The child picks the reply that fits, puts a scene's lines in order and speaks one part. Judging honestly what you can already do in English and setting a next goal. Words, greetings and customs for special days in England. Lesson 1 brings birthdays (Happy birthday!, cake, candles, presents, balloons) and Christmas (Merry Christmas!, Christmas tree, Father Christmas, the stocking, presents on the morning of 25 December rather than on Heiligabend). Lesson 2 brings Easter (Easter eggs, the Easter bunny, the egg hunt), Halloween (witch, ghost, pumpkin, Trick or treat!) and Bonfire Night on 5 November (bonfire, fireworks), and ends with the skill check. The child reads and completes short greetings for cards.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Geburtstag und Weihnachten | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Ostern, Halloween und Bonfire Night | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

## Geschichten hören (Listening to stories)

`de-DE.fl.en-stories` teaches `fl.listening.stories` in de-DE, lernjahr-1. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Short picture stories read aloud in English: a hungry mouse, a snowman, a kite in a tree, a birthday. Lesson 1 listens for the gist: who is in the story, what it is about and how it ends. Lesson 2 listens for the order of events with first, then and in the end, puts the pictures in order, and ends with the skill check. Questions are in German, so the task tests listening, not reading.

`de-DE.fl.en-time` teaches `fl.themes.time` in de-DE, lernjahr-2. Ages 8-10, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Asking for the time and saying full and half hours. Lesson 1 brings "What time is it? ## Adjektive vor dem Nomen (Adjectives before the noun)

`de-DE.lit-de.adjectives-agreement` teaches `lit-de.adjectives.agreement` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

An adjective before a noun gets an ending that fits the noun: der rote Ball, ein roter Ball, die roten Bälle. Lesson 1 uses der, die, das (der große Hund, die große Katze, das große Haus: always -e); lesson 2 uses ein and eine (ein großer Hund, eine große Katze, ein großes Haus) and the plural (die großen Hunde), and ends with the skill check. ## Balladen (Ballads)

`de-DE.lit-de.ballads` teaches `lit-de.texts-media.ballads` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 15 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

A Ballade is a poem that tells an exciting story. Lesson 1 reads two short ballads written for Twist (Jan und das Lamm, Grete im Leuchtturm), tells a ballad from a poem without a story and from a story without Verse, finds who speaks, the Höhepunkt and the order of events. Lesson 2 reads Theodor Fontane's "Herr von Ribbeck auf Ribbeck im Havelland" (first and last two Strophen quoted, the middle retold), translates its Plattdeutsch lines, says why Ribbeck takes a pear into his grave, looks at the first Strophe of Goethe's "Erlkönig", and ends with the skill check. Fontane is quoted word for word from the Cotta edition of 1905 and Goethe from Goethes Werke (Bertelsmann), as on Wikisource, in their original spelling (Thurme, faßt).


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Gedichte, die Geschichten erzählen | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Herr von Ribbeck | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Children write Nomen and the first word of a sentence with a capital letter. Lesson 1 finds the Nomen inside sentences with the Begleiter test (can der, die or das go in front?) and picks the capital or small first letter for a word in a sentence. Lesson 2 adds the start of a sentence: the first word is always written big, also after a Punkt, even when it is not a Nomen; it finds every word that needs a capital in a sentence written all small, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Nomen im Satz schreibt man groß | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Der Satzanfang ist groß | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Reading what characters feel and think. ## Sätze abschreiben (Copying sentences)

`de-DE.lit-de.copy-sentences` teaches `lit-de.handwriting.copy-sentences` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children copy short sentences without mistakes, with the same Abschreiben steps as for words: read, remember, write, compare. A sentence needs three more things than a word: a gap a finger wide between the words, a capital letter at the start and a Punkt at the end. Lesson 1 copies three-word sentences word by word; lesson 2 remembers two words at a time, checks copies for all three things and ends with the skill check. The app does not yet compare the child's strokes with the model, so the writing tasks are practice and the check rests on the compare, remember and order tasks.

`de-DE.lit-de.copy-words` teaches `lit-de.handwriting.copy-words` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children copy words without mistakes by the Abschreiben steps a class learns: read the word, remember it, write it from memory, then compare. The app does not yet compare the child's strokes with the model, so the writing tasks are practice and the check rests on the compare and remember tasks.

`de-DE.lit-de.digital-communication` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.digital-communication` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

How people talk in messages, chats and video calls, and the rules that hold there. Lesson 1 looks at chat language: short and close to speech, Abkürzungen (LG, vllt, kA), Emojis that show a feeling, and messages that are easy to misread because the reader cannot hear your voice; it sets a chat with friends against a message to the teacher. All chats are written for Twist; each emoji is also described in the spoken text.

## Informationen im Text finden (Finding information in a text)

`de-DE.lit-de.find-information` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.find-information` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Finding answers that stand in the text. Lesson 1 reads short stories, takes the question word as a guide (Wer, Wo, Wann, Wie viele) and finds the sentence with the answer; lesson 2 reads short Sachtexte about animals, sorts what the text says from what it does not say, records facts in gaps, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Die Antwort steht im Text | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Informationen in Sachtexten | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: true_false 10, highlight_in_text 6, multiple_choice 19, fill_in_blank 3, sorting 4. Builds on: `lit-de.comprehension.read-precisely`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Erste Sätze lesen (Reading first sentences)

`de-DE.lit-de.first-sentences` teaches `lit-de.decoding.first-sentences` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Reading a short sentence to the end and finding the picture that fits. Lesson 1: one sentence of three to five words; the last word decides (Der Ball ist rot, Hier sind drei Eier), so the child reads every word before choosing; the child also builds a sentence from word cards. Lesson 2: sentences that differ in one word (Hund, Mund), two sentences that only together name a thing (Es ist gelb. Es ist am Himmel.), and checking a sentence against a picture; it ends with the skill check. Words use only letters taught so far (up to Eu, Ch and J) and the first sight words. The voice never reads the sentence the child has to read.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Ein Satz und ein Bild | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Genau lesen | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: image_multiple_choice 21, audio_choice 1, true_false 9, ordering 6, multiple_choice 6. Builds on: `lit-de.decoding.word-picture`, `lit-de.fluency.sight-words-easy`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Erste Texte lesen (Reading first texts)

`de-DE.lit-de.first-texts` teaches `lit-de.decoding.first-texts` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Reading a short text of three to four sentences and answering questions about it. Lesson 1: the answer stands in one sentence; the question word (wer, was, wo, wie viele) says what to look for, and the child goes back to the text to find it. Lesson 2: reading more carefully, where a text names more than one thing of a kind (gern and heute, Papa and Ida) and where a statement has to be checked word by word against the text; it ends with the skill check on a new text. All texts are written for Twist; the voice asks the question but never reads the text.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Die Antwort steht im Text | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Genau nachlesen | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: image_multiple_choice 15, true_false 9, multiple_choice 16, text_input 2. Builds on: `lit-de.decoding.read-and-do`, `lit-de.decoding.longer-words`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Fremdwörter (Borrowed words)

`de-DE.lit-de.foreign-words` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.foreign-words` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Words German took from other languages. Lesson 1 spots Fremdwörter in a sentence, sorts them by the language they come from (Englisch, Französisch, Italienisch), learns that they are often not spoken as they are written and so must be learnt as Merkwörter, and meets Lehnwörter that came so long ago that they look German (Fenster, Mauer). Lesson 2 finds the meaning of a Fremdwort from the sentence, swaps in a German word, reads the Herkunftsangabe in a dictionary entry, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Woher kommen die Wörter? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Was bedeutet das Fremdwort? | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 27, fill_in_blank 1, sorting 4, true_false 5, highlight_in_text 2, matching 3. Builds on: `lit-de.spelling.ph-v-foreign`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Kleine Wörter blitzschnell lesen (Reading small words in a flash)

`de-DE.lit-de.function-words` teaches `lit-de.fluency.function-words` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Blitzlesen with the small words that hold a sentence together. Lesson 1 takes the Begleiter der, die, das and den; lesson 2 takes ein, eine, auf, nicht and sind, finds them inside short sentences, and ends with the skill check. As in the first Blitzlesen lessons, a word is shown for two seconds and covered, and the options are always words that look alike (der and den, die and sie, auf and aus, ein and eine), because that is where a quick look goes wrong.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. der, die, das, den | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. ein, eine, auf, nicht, sind | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 20, audio_choice 8, true_false 8, highlight_in_text 6. Builds on: `lit-de.fluency.sight-words-easy`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Lückenwörter (Gap words)

`de-DE.lit-de.gap-words` teaches `lit-de.spelling.gap-words` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children fill the gaps in words and build words from syllables and letters. Lesson 1 fills one missing letter or letter group, including the ones that are one sound but more letters (sch, ei, au, ch, eu): Fi_ becomes Fisch. Lesson 2 builds words from syllable cards with one card too many and from letter cards, and ends with the skill check. The strategy is always the same: say the word slowly and listen where the gap is.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Was fehlt? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Wörter bauen | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: fill_in_blank 27, ordering 15. Builds on: `lit-de.spelling.write-by-sound`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Die passende Überschrift (The right heading)

`de-DE.lit-de.headings` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.headings` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Finding the heading that fits a short text. Lesson 1 learns that a Überschrift says in a few words what the whole text is about, and picks it from three: the right one, one that names only a detail, and one about something else; lesson 2 goes the other way, finds the text for a heading, matches headings to short texts, says whether a heading fits, and ends with the skill check. All texts are written for Twist.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Was passt darüber? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Überschrift und Text | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 21, image_multiple_choice 8, true_false 9, matching 4. Builds on: `lit-de.decoding.first-texts`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Redewendungen und Sprichwörter (Idioms and proverbs)

`de-DE.lit-de.idioms` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.idioms` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Sayings that mean more than their words. Lesson 1 takes Redewendungen (Tomaten auf den Augen haben, jemandem einen Bären aufbinden, Schwein haben, auf dem Schlauch stehen), sets the literal picture against what is meant, and matches each to its meaning; lesson 2 adds Sprichwörter (Übung macht den Meister, Lügen haben kurze Beine), tells a Sprichwort from a Redewendung, picks the saying that fits a scene, and ends with the skill check. All scenes are written for Twist.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Was ist gemeint? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Sprichwörter | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 24, matching 3, true_false 6, sorting 6, fill_in_blank 3. Builds on: `lit-de.comprehension.infer`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Unbekannte Wörter verstehen (Working out unknown words)

`de-DE.lit-de.infer-words` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.infer-words` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Working out what a new word means. Lesson 1 finds the clue in the same sentence or the next one (Der Fuchs ist flink. Kein Hund kann ihn fangen.) and the word in the text that means nearly the same; lesson 2 reads on for the clue, uses a known word inside a long one (verschlafen, Federtasche), knows when to look a word up and under which letter, and ends with the skill check.

|---|---|---|---|

## Zwischen den Zeilen lesen (Reading between the lines)

`de-DE.lit-de.infer` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.infer` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Drawing conclusions that a text does not state. Lesson 1 reads short texts and works out from clues what the text does not say (the weather, a place, a feeling); lesson 2 links two places in a text to answer a question, tells what stands in the text from what you can work out, and ends with the skill check. Every text is written for Twist.

## Anlaute hören (Hearing first sounds)

`de-DE.lit-de.initial-sound` teaches `lit-de.phonological.initial-sound` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children hear the first sound of a word (Sonne starts with sss) and find words that start with the same sound. There are no letters yet: the sounds are spoken, and every picture says its word. Lesson 1 listens for one sound at a time; lesson 2 pairs and sorts words by their first sound, finds the one that starts differently, and ends with the skill check.

## Ein Interview führen (Holding an interview)

`de-DE.lit-de.interview` teaches `lit-de.speaking.interview` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Planning an interview and noting the answers. Lesson 1 prepares questions: open W-Fragen instead of Ja-Nein-Fragen, questions that fit the goal of the interview, and a polite start and end; lesson 2 holds the interview with a baker, follows up with a Nachfrage, notes the answers as Stichwörter, and ends with the skill check. The people and answers are written for Twist. The speak tasks run as the record-and-compare fallback and are never scored.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |

| 2. Nachfragen und notieren | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: fill_in_blank 1, multiple_choice 20, sorting 4, ordering 4, speak 6, true_false 5, matching 2. Builds on: `lit-de.speaking.argue`, `lit-de.sentences.questions`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Schreibschrift, große Buchstaben (Joined script, capital letters)

`de-DE.lit-de.joined-capitals` teaches `lit-de.handwriting.joined-capitals` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children write the capital letters in Schreibschrift and go on from them into the small letters of the word. Lesson 1 sets out where the capitals live (from the line up to the Dach) and when a word starts with one (Satzanfang, Nomen, Namen); lesson 2 joins a capital to the rest of the word, and where a capital is not joined, sets the next letter close beside it so the word stays together; it ends with the skill check. Which capitals join depends on the school's Ausgangsschrift, so the tasks never name one and only say that some capitals join and some do not. The player still draws the writing lines in print, so no task says the child can see a join. Word options for "which word do you write with a capital" are shown in small letters, so the capital does not give the answer away.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Die großen Buchstaben | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Groß und klein zusammen | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 17, tracing 17, true_false 7, highlight_in_text 3. Builds on: `lit-de.handwriting.joined-lowercase`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Flüssig und leserlich schreiben (Writing fluently and legibly)

`de-DE.lit-de.joined-fluent` teaches `lit-de.handwriting.joined-fluent` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children write words and sentences in Schreibschrift so that others can read them, and without stopping after every letter. Lesson 1 looks at what makes writing leserlich: clear letters (an open a turns Hand into Hund), letters of the same size sitting on the line, and a finger gap between words; lesson 2 is about writing flüssig: a loose grip, not pressing too hard, remembering a group of words and writing it in one go, and checking the writing at the end; it ends with the skill check. Copying on writing lines is the practice; the check asks what makes writing easy to read and to write. The models on the writing lines are still drawn in print, so the copy tasks ask the child to write them in Schreibschrift, as in a school Übertragung.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Leserlich schreiben | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Flüssig schreiben | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 17, tracing 15, true_false 9, sorting 3. Builds on: `lit-de.handwriting.joined-capitals`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Schreibschrift, kleine Buchstaben (Joined script, lowercase letters)

`de-DE.lit-de.joined-lowercase` teaches `lit-de.handwriting.joined-lowercase` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children write the small letters in Schreibschrift and join them, so a word is written in one go without lifting the pen. Lesson 1 joins at the bottom: most small letters end low and the joining stroke runs up into the next letter (im, mein, lila); lesson 2 joins at the top after o, w, v and b, sets i-Punkte, Umlaut-Punkte and t-Striche once the word is finished, and ends with the skill check. Tracing and copying on writing lines teach the joins; choice, sorting and true or false tasks check what the child knows about them. No task names one Ausgangsschrift: the joins taught (most letters end low, o, w, v and b end high) hold for the joined scripts German schools use. The player still draws the writing lines in print, so no task says the child can see a join; the spoken text describes the movement instead.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Unten verbunden | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Oben verbunden | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 19, tracing 15, true_false 6, sorting 3, highlight_in_text 1. Builds on: `lit-de.handwriting.copy-sentences`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Schlüsselwörter finden (Finding key words)

`de-DE.lit-de.key-words` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.key-words` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Schlüsselwörter are the few words that carry what matters. Lesson 1 finds them in single sentences, tells them from small words (der, und, im) and marks only the words that answer a question; lesson 2 finds the key words that show what a short Sachtext is about, takes the key word of a question into the text, and ends with the skill check.

|---|---|---|---|

Task types: multiple_choice 14, true_false 10, highlight_in_text 12, sorting 6. Builds on: `lit-de.comprehension.find-information`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Schreiben auf der Tastatur (Typing on a keyboard)

`de-DE.lit-de.keyboard` teaches `lit-de.handwriting.keyboard` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Typing a short text on a German keyboard and putting it right on the screen. Lesson 1 types words and sentences: the Umschalttaste for a capital, one press of the Leertaste between words, and the keys for ä, ö, ü and ß; lesson 2 adds the Satzzeichen (Punkt and Komma on their own keys, Fragezeichen and Ausrufezeichen with the Umschalttaste), corrects with the Rücktaste and the cursor, reads the red wavy line as a hint and not as the last word, and ends with the skill check. The child chooses keys, orders key presses and finds typing mistakes in short texts; a typing field in the app would let the child type for real.

Task types: multiple_choice 30, ordering 5, true_false 6, highlight_in_text 3. Builds on: `lit-de.handwriting.copy-sentences`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Wie Sprache wirkt (How language works on people)

`de-DE.lit-de.language-effect` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.language-effect` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The same thing can be said in different ways, and each way works differently on the listener. Lesson 1 sets a polite request against a command, sorts friendly from rude and hurtful sentences, asks how the other person feels, and turns a reproach into an Ich-Botschaft. Lesson 2 sets sachlich against werbend, finds the words that advertise (super, am besten, nur heute), learns that a reason convinces more than shouting, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Freundlich oder verletzend? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Sachlich, werbend, überzeugend | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 28, sorting 7, true_false 5, highlight_in_text 2. Builds on: `lit-de.comprehension.infer`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Mit Sprache spielen (Playing with language)

`de-DE.lit-de.language-play` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.language-play` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Playing with words and sounds. Lesson 1 tells real words from Quatschwörter, finds the Quatschsatz, swaps the first sound of a word to make a new one (Maus, Haus) and makes up Quatschreime; lesson 2 swaps the syllables of a word (Hase, Seha), turns a Quatschwort back into the real word, swaps the parts of a compound and sees the meaning change (Baumhaus, Hausbaum), and ends with the skill check. Speak tasks let the child say a Quatschsatz or a swapped word aloud; they are practice only. Every Quatschwort and Quatschsatz is made up for Twist.

Task types: true_false 8, image_multiple_choice 11, sorting 3, multiple_choice 16, speak 5, ordering 3. Builds on: `lit-de.phonological.rhymes`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Sprachen und Dialekte vergleichen (Comparing languages and dialects)

`de-DE.lit-de.languages` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.languages` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Comparing German with other languages children may speak at home or in class, and with its own Dialekte. Lesson 1 finds words that sound alike across languages (Nacht, night, notte, noche; Haus, house), greets in Englisch, Türkisch, Polnisch, Italienisch and Spanisch, and notices what works differently (the for der, die and das in English, no such article in Turkish, capital nouns only in German, Arabic written from right to left). Lesson 2 takes Dialekte: Brötchen as Semmel, Schrippe, Rundstück or Weckle, Moin and Servus, Sonnabend and Samstag, and ends with the skill check. Every word is shown in Latin letters; hearing the other languages waits for voices in those languages.

|---|---|---|---|

Task types: multiple_choice 21, matching 7, sorting 6, true_false 9. Builds on: . Misconceptions addressed: 2.

`de-DE.lit-de.lengthen-dt` teaches `lit-de.spelling-strategies.lengthen-dt` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

At the end of a word d and t sound the same. Lesson 1 makes a noun longer with its plural (Hund, Hunde; Brot, Brote) to hear which letter it is; lesson 2 does the same for Adjektive with a noun (rund, ein runder Ball; bunt, ein buntes Bild), and ends with the skill check.

## C, Y, X, äu, ck und ß (C, Y, X, äu, ck and ß)

`de-DE.lit-de.letters-c-y-x-aeu-ck-ss` teaches `lit-de.letters.c-y-x-aeu-ck-ss` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The last letter group of Klasse 1: rare letters and spellings the child meets in reading. Lesson 1: ck is read as k and comes after a short vowel (Socke, Glocke); ß is read as s and is not a B (Fuß, groß). Lesson 2: äu sounds like eu and comes from au (Maus, Mäuse; Baum, Bäume); C is often read as k (Clown), Y at the end of a word as i (Baby, Pony), X as ks (Hexe, Taxi). Lesson 3 reads words with all six and ends with the skill check. Choosing between k and ck by rule is left to the later spelling skill; here ck is offered against k and kk in short familiar words.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. ck und ß | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. äu, C, Y und X | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 3. Wörter mit seltenen Buchstaben | no | 22 | warm-up, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

The third letter group in Fibel order. One lesson each for E, N, D and U: hear the sound, find it in words, see and trace the letter, practised in three stages. Each lesson sets the new sound against its nearest neighbour: E against I, N against M, D against T, U against O (Mund and Mond). ## ng, nk, Qu und V (ng, nk, Qu and V)

`de-DE.lit-de.letters-ng-nk-qu-v` teaches `lit-de.letters.ng-nk-qu-v` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The letter group after St, Sp and Pf. Lesson 1: ng is one sound made at the back of the mouth, with no g to hear (Schlange, Junge, Ring), and nk is that sound with a k after it (Anker, Bank); the child hears which one it is and keeps ng apart from a plain n. Lesson 2: Qu always comes as Q and u together and sounds kw (Qualle, Quadrat); V mostly sounds f (Vogel, Vater) and sometimes w (Pullover), so V words are learnt as Merkwörter. Lesson 3 reads and sorts words with all four and ends with the skill check.

## Zuhören und Anweisungen folgen (Listening and following instructions)

`de-DE.lit-de.listen-follow` teaches `lit-de.speaking.listen-follow` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Listening to a spoken instruction to the end and doing exactly what it says. Lesson 1 listens for the two details that matter (the red ball, not the red crayon) and for two steps in a row; lesson 2 follows three steps with zuerst, dann and zum Schluss, counts the steps, asks again when something was not understood, and ends with the skill check. Every instruction is spoken; the child answers with action pictures, objects or short spoken sentences. Asking again aloud is practised with speak tasks and not marked.

## Medienbeiträge gestalten (Making media)

`de-DE.lit-de.make-media` teaches `lit-de.texts-media.make-media` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Planning a small media product of your own. The app cannot judge a real slideshow or recording, so the lessons teach and check what goes into a good one. Lesson 1 plans a Präsentation and a Plakat: the steps in order, Stichwörter instead of whole sentences on a Folie, one topic per Folie, large writing and a clear Überschrift, and pictures you may use with their Quelle. Lesson 2 plans a Hörgeschichte: Erzähler, Figuren, Geräusche and Musik, how to make a sound at home, a Drehbuch in order, recording tips and asking before you record or photograph someone; it ends with the skill check. Hearing real sound effects waits for effect sounds in the bank; here they are described in words.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Präsentation und Plakat | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Eine Hörgeschichte planen | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 21, true_false 9, ordering 7, sorting 5. Builds on: `lit-de.texts-media.media-use`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Medien bewusst nutzen (Using media wisely)

`de-DE.lit-de.media-use` teaches `lit-de.texts-media.media-use` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Thinking about the media children use every day: audio stories, videos, games and apps. Lesson 1 asks what a medium wants (informieren, unterhalten, verkaufen) and finds advertising, also where it hides: an Anzeige at the top of a search, a product praised in a video. Lesson 2 reads the Altersfreigabe, sees that a free game can still cost money, keeps personal data private, checks a chain message before passing it on, and says what helps when a video was frightening or screen time ran long; it ends with the skill check. All pages, videos and messages are described in text and written for Twist.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Was wollen Medien von mir? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Klug mit Medien umgehen | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 25, sorting 7, true_false 10. Builds on: `lit-de.texts-media.digital-texts`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Merkwörter (Words to learn by heart)

`de-DE.lit-de.memory-words` teaches `lit-de.spelling-strategies.memory-words` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Some spellings cannot be heard or worked out and must be learnt. Lesson 1 takes words with the Dehnungs-h (Uhr, Kuh, Stuhl) and with a double Selbstlaut (Boot, Meer, Haar) and how to learn them; lesson 2 takes the Wochentage, the Monate and the Zahlwörter, and ends with the skill check.

## Stichwörter notieren (Taking notes)

`de-DE.lit-de.notes` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.notes` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Noting key points from an information text and retelling it from the notes. Lesson 1 finds the words worth noting in a sentence and tells a Stichwort from a whole sentence; lesson 2 fills a note sheet under headings, puts notes in order, retells a text from its notes, and ends with the skill check. The short Sachtexte about animals and plants are written for Twist and use well-established facts. Writing notes freely waits for a free text task type; here the child chooses and places notes.

## Die vier Fälle (The four cases)

`de-DE.lit-de.noun-cases` teaches `lit-de.nouns.cases` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The case questions find the case of a noun: Wer oder was? (Nominativ), Wessen? (Genitiv), Wem? (Dativ), Wen oder was? (Akkusativ). Lesson 1 finds the Nominativ and the Akkusativ; lesson 2 adds the Dativ; lesson 3 adds the Genitiv, puts nouns into a given case and ends with the skill check. The article shows the case: der Hund, des Hundes, dem Hund, den Hund.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Wer oder was? Wen oder was? | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Wem? | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 3. Wessen? Und alle vier Fälle | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

## Nomen mit -ung, -heit, -keit und -nis (Nouns ending in -ung, -heit, -keit and -nis)

`de-DE.lit-de.noun-endings` teaches `lit-de.nouns.endings` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Words ending in -ung, -heit, -keit and -nis are nouns, and so they are written with a capital. Lesson 1 makes nouns from verbs with -ung (wohnen, die Wohnung) and from adjectives with -heit and -keit (frei, die Freiheit; freundlich, die Freundlichkeit); lesson 2 adds -nis, finds such nouns in a sentence written all in small letters, and ends with the skill check.

| 2. -nis und Nomen im Satz | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

## der, die, das und ein, eine (Articles: der, die, das and ein, eine)

`de-DE.lit-de.nouns-articles` teaches `lit-de.nouns.articles` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children find the Begleiter of a Nomen: der, die or das, and then ein or eine. The trick a class uses is to say the word aloud with each Begleiter and listen for the one that sounds right. Lesson 1 practises der, die and das with pictures and short word cards; lesson 2 adds ein and eine, links der and das to ein and die to eine, and ends with the skill check.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1. der, die, das | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. ein und eine | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 16, true_false 9, fill_in_blank 9, sorting 6, matching 2. Builds on: `lit-de.nouns.recognise`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Einzahl und Mehrzahl (Singular and plural)

`de-DE.lit-de.nouns-plural` teaches `lit-de.nouns.plural` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children tell Einzahl from Mehrzahl and form the Mehrzahl of common nouns. Lesson 1 hears and sees one thing against many (der Hund, die Hunde), learns that the Begleiter in the Mehrzahl is always die, and that die alone does not make a Mehrzahl (die Katze is one cat). Lesson 2 forms the Mehrzahl with the counting trick (ein Stern, zwei Sterne): endings e, en, n and er, the a that turns into ä (der Apfel, die Äpfel), and words that stay the same (der Löffel, die Löffel); it ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Eins oder viele? | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Die Mehrzahl bilden | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 17, image_multiple_choice 4, true_false 6, matching 4, sorting 2, fill_in_blank 9. Builds on: `lit-de.nouns.articles`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Nomen erkennen (Recognising nouns)

`de-DE.lit-de.nouns-recognise` teaches `lit-de.nouns.recognise` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children learn that Nomen are the names for people, animals, plants and things, and that a Nomen always starts with a capital letter. Lesson 1 sorts pictures into Menschen, Tiere, Pflanzen and Dinge and picks the Nomen out of word groups, written in GROSSBUCHSTABEN so the capital letter does not give the Nomen away; lesson 2 uses the capital letter: find every Nomen in a short sentence, pick the right spelling, and ends with the skill check.

| 2. Nomen schreibt man groß | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

## Kurze Wörter lesen (Reading one-syllable words)

`de-DE.lit-de.one-syllable-words` teaches `lit-de.decoding.one-syllable-words` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children read short words of one syllable, made only from the letters they know so far (L, O, A, M, I, ie, S, T, E, N, D, U): Tal, Mus, Tee, Lied in lesson 1, then words that end in two consonants (Mond, Mund, Nest, Ast, Sand) in lesson 2. They hear a word and find it written, read a word and find its picture, answer a yes or no question about what a word means, and tell apart words that differ in one letter. Lesson 2 ends with the skill check.

## Meinung schriftlich begründen (Giving reasons for an opinion in writing)

`de-DE.lit-de.opinion` teaches `lit-de.writing.opinion` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The knowledge behind an opinion text. Lesson 1 builds a Buchtipp and a short Meinungstext (Meinung, Argumente mit Beispiel, Schluss), tells a real argument from a sentence that is not one, and links arguments with denn, außerdem and deshalb; lesson 2 writes a letter with a request to the head teacher or the mayor (Anrede, Bitte, Argumente, Gruß), chooses the argument that convinces this reader, and ends with the skill check. Writing a whole letter freely waits for a free text task type; here the child sorts, orders, chooses and fills. Every letter and book is written for Twist.

|---|---|---|---|

| 2. Ein Brief mit einer Bitte | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

## Geschichten ordnen (Putting a story in order)

`de-DE.lit-de.order-story` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.order-story` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Putting the parts of a short story in order. Lesson 1 reads a story of three short sentences and lays its pictures in order, finds what happens first and what is missing in the middle, and learns the order words zuerst, dann and zum Schluss; lesson 2 orders sentence cards with and without order words, uses cause and effect (first the cup falls over, then the table is wet), tells the beginning from the end, and ends with the skill check. All stories are written for Twist.

## Bildzeichen verstehen (Understanding signs and symbols)

`de-DE.lit-de.pictograms` teaches `lit-de.texts-media.pictograms` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Reading the picture signs of everyday life. Lesson 1 reads the Bildzeichen that tell a child in a workbook what to do (pencil: write, scissors: cut out, ear: listen, eye: look closely, mouth: say it, crayon: colour in, open book: read) and learns that a sign means an action, not just the thing on it; lesson 2 reads signs outside: the traffic light (one picture with the red man lit, one with the green man lit), the signs for the bus stop, cycle path, toilet, emergency exit and a café, and a red line across a picture that means "not allowed", and ends with the skill check.

`de-DE.lit-de.plan-texts` teaches `lit-de.writing.plan` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Planning before writing. Lesson 1 collects ideas in a Cluster: the Thema in the middle, Stichwörter around it, questions that bring ideas (Wer? Wo? Was passiert?), and only ideas that fit the Thema; lesson 2 crosses out ideas that do not fit, turns the rest into a Schreibplan with Anfang, Mitte and Schluss, and ends with the skill check. Drawing a Cluster freely waits for a free text task type; here the child chooses, sorts and orders ideas.

## Buchstaben schreiben (Writing print letters)

`de-DE.lit-de.print-letters` teaches `lit-de.handwriting.print-letters` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children write print letters in the Häuschen of four lines: small letters like a, o, m live in the Erdgeschoss, tall ones like l and d reach the Dach, and every capital starts at the top line. Each letter starts at the green dot and is written from top to bottom. Lesson 1 sets out the Häuschen with the letters already known (L, O, A, M); lesson 2 adds i, t, e, n, u and d and ends with the skill check. Tracing and copying carry the writing; short choice tasks ask where a letter starts and which floors it uses.

|---|---|---|---|

Reading a short Arbeitsanweisung and doing what it says. The instruction is on screen and the voice only says "Lies die Aufgabe und mach, was dort steht", so the child has to read it. Lesson 2: the usual instruction words (kreuze an, kreise ein, streiche durch, unterstreiche, schreibe ab), two-step instructions with zuerst and dann, sorting by an instruction, and the skill check. Paper actions such as circling are checked as knowing what the word asks for.

Reading word by word and checking that it makes sense. Lesson 1 judges single sentences (Kann das sein?), taps the word that does not fit and picks the word that belongs there; lesson 2 does the same in short stories and finds the sentence that does not belong to the story, and ends with the skill check.

## Lesestrategien (Reading strategies)

`de-DE.lit-de.reading-strategies` teaches `lit-de.comprehension.strategies` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

A plan for reading a Sachtext: before, during and after. Lesson 1 asks questions from the title before reading and checks understanding while reading (read again, work out a word from the sentence, look it up); lesson 2 sums up the main points after reading, leaves out details, checks the questions from before, and ends with the skill check. Every text is written for Twist.

| 1. Vor dem Lesen und beim Lesen | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |

## Gedichte auswendig lernen und vortragen (Learning poems by heart and reciting them)

`de-DE.lit-de.recite-poems` teaches `lit-de.texts-media.recite` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Learning a short poem by heart and saying it with expression. Lesson 1 learns a poem stanza by stanza and line by line, uses the rhyme words to recall the next line, fills gaps and puts lines in order; lesson 2 picks the word to stress, matches the voice to the mood (quiet, cheerful, calm), sorts good and poor ways of reciting, and ends with the skill check. Speak tasks let the child say lines aloud and compare with the model; they are practice only. Every poem is written for Twist.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Strophe für Strophe | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |

## Sich aufnehmen und besser sprechen (Recording yourself and speaking better)

`de-DE.lit-de.record-voice` teaches `lit-de.speaking.record-voice` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Listening to your own voice and making it clearer. Lesson 1 learns three things to listen for (laut genug, deutlich, ein gutes Tempo with pauses at the full stop) and works out what a speaker should practise from what a recording sounds like; lesson 2 follows the steps aufnehmen, anhören, prüfen, verbessern, nochmal aufnehmen, chooses the next thing to work on, and ends with the skill check. Every lesson records the child's voice in speak tasks, which run as the record-and-compare fallback and are never scored; the check asks only what the child knows about it.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Laut, deutlich, nicht zu schnell | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Aufnehmen, anhören, verbessern | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

## Einen Bericht schreiben (Writing a report)

`de-DE.lit-de.report` teaches `lit-de.writing.report` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 14 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The knowledge behind a factual report. Lesson 1 answers the W-Fragen (Wer? Was? Wann? Wo? Wie? Warum?), finds the W-Frage a report leaves open and puts events in the order they happened; lesson 2 keeps a report sachlich (no feelings, no suspense, no opinion), writes it in the Präteritum, tells a report from a story, and ends with the skill check. Writing a whole report freely waits for a free text task type; here the child sorts, orders, chooses and fills. Every event is written for Twist.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Die W-Fragen beantworten | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |

## Informationen suchen und prüfen (Finding and checking information)

`de-DE.lit-de.research` teaches `lit-de.texts-media.research` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Looking up an answer to a question and checking whether it can be trusted. Lesson 1 turns a question into search words, finds the entry in a children's encyclopedia by its first letter, picks the search result that fits and puts the research steps in order; lesson 2 checks whether a page is reliable (who made it, advertising, a second source), spots the source that is wrong, notes the answer with its source, and ends with the skill check. Search results are shown as text lists; the child does not use a live browser.

`de-DE.lit-de.revise-correctness` teaches `lit-de.writing.revise-correctness` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Checking a finished text for spelling, punctuation and grammar. Lesson 1 works through a checklist (capital at the start, capital nouns, a mark at the end, a verb that fits) on short sentences; lesson 2 checks on the computer, chooses the right suggestion of the spell checker, finds the mistakes it cannot see (real words in the wrong place, commas, Redezeichen), and ends with the skill check. Correcting a whole text of the child's own waits for a free text task type; here the child finds, chooses and fills.

## Texte überarbeiten (Revising writing)

`de-DE.lit-de.revise-texts` teaches `lit-de.writing.revise` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Making a text better with others and with a checklist. Lesson 1 runs a Schreibkonferenz: one child reads, the others say what they like and give a friendly, exact tip, the writer decides; it matches problems in a text to the checklist questions (Überschrift, Reihenfolge, Satzanfänge, genaue Wörter, versteht man alles?); lesson 2 changes repeated Satzanfänge, finds more exact words, puts sentences in order, spots an unclear er or sie, and ends with the skill check. Revising a child's own text waits for a free text task type; here the child chooses, orders and matches.

## Blitzlesen (Flash reading)

`de-DE.lit-de.sight-words-easy` teaches `lit-de.fluency.sight-words-easy` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 9 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children learn to know short frequent words at a glance, the way a Blitzlesen card game trains them: the word appears for two seconds and is covered, and the child says which word it was. Lesson 1 works with und, ist, im, am, in and an; lesson 2 with mit, da, du, so and es, and ends with the skill check. Words that look alike (im, in; am, an; um, und) are always set side by side, because that is where a quick look goes wrong.

Recognising longer words of the Grundwortschatz at a glance. Taking information from simple tables, plans and charts. All tables and charts are written for Twist.

## Die Anlauttabelle (The sound chart)

`de-DE.lit-de.sound-chart` teaches `lit-de.letters.sound-chart` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Using an Anlauttabelle before the letters are taught one by one. Each field of the chart is a letter with a picture whose word starts with that letter's sound (M wie Maus). Lesson 1: hear a sound or a word, find the chart picture that starts the same, and with it the letter; keep the sound apart from the letter name (Elefant does not start with L) and from sounds later in the word. Lesson 2 goes through a whole short word sound by sound (Hut: H wie Hund, U wie Uhr, T wie Tisch), puts the chart pictures in order and ends with the skill check. The chart is never on screen as a whole: bins and options carry its fields, and tapping one says it ("M wie Maus").

## das oder dass? (das or dass?)

`de-DE.lit-de.spelling-das-dass` teaches `lit-de.spelling.das-dass` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Telling das from dass with the Ersatzprobe. Lesson 1 shows das as a Begleiter (das Haus), standing alone (Das schmeckt lecker.) and as a word you can swap for welches (Das Bild, das ich male); when dieses, jenes or welches does not fit, it is dass, which a few tasks set as a contrast. Lesson 2 shows dass as a Bindewort after a comma that cannot be swapped (Ich weiß, dass du gern malst.), mixes both, and ends with the skill check.

## Gesprochene und geschriebene Sprache (Spoken and written language)

`de-DE.lit-de.spoken-written` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.spoken-written` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

How we speak and how we write. Lesson 1 sets spoken German against written German: short forms (hab, is, nich, 'ne), filler words (äh, halt, also), unfinished sentences and the Perfekt in speech, full sentences and the Präteritum in a written story. Lesson 2 rewrites Umgangssprache in Standardsprache (super krass, voll müde, Kumpel), decides where each fits (with a friend, in a letter to the Bürgermeisterin, in a school report), meets a few words from Dialekte, and ends with the skill check.

Giving a text a clear shape, so a reader finds their way at a glance. Lesson 1 lays out a text by hand: a heading at the top (often in the middle or underlined with a ruler; classes differ, so only "at the top" is marked), a Rand left free on both sides, a new Absatz on a new line whenever something new begins, and a clean line through a mistake instead of scribbling; lesson 2 does the same on the computer: a bigger or bold heading set in the middle with a button, the Eingabetaste for a new Absatz instead of many spaces, one easy-to-read font, and ends with the skill check. The child judges and fixes layouts shown as text and described in words; the app does not draw a page yet.

## Verben beugen (Verb forms for each person)

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-conjugate` teaches `lit-de.verbs.conjugate` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Das Verb passt sich an, wer etwas tut: ich spiele, du spielst, er spielt. Lesson 1 builds the forms for ich, du and er, sie, es from the word stem and its ending; lesson 2 adds wir, ihr and sie, puts the whole table together, and ends with the skill check. Only regular verbs whose stem does not end in t, d, s or z, so every ending is the plain one.

## Die Zukunft (The future)

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-future` teaches `lit-de.verbs.future` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The Futur says what will happen: Ich werde spielen. Lesson 1 builds it from a form of werden and the Grundform at the end of the sentence (ich werde, du wirst, er wird, wir werden); lesson 2 shows that German often uses the Präsens with a time word for the future (Morgen fahre ich zu Oma.), finds sentences about the future, and ends with the skill check.

## Die Aufforderungsform (The imperative)

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-imperative` teaches `lit-de.verbs.imperative` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Verbs in the imperative tell someone what to do (Komm! Kommt!). Lesson 1 forms the imperative for one person (komm, lauf), including the verbs that change e to i (lies, gib, nimm) and verbs whose front part moves to the end (Räum auf!); lesson 2 forms it for several people (kommt, lest) and for polite address (Kommen Sie!), sets the exclamation mark, and ends with the skill check.

## Das Perfekt (The present perfect)

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-perfect` teaches `lit-de.verbs.perfect` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The Perfekt is the past we use when we speak: Ich habe gespielt. It has two parts, haben or sein and the Partizip (gespielt, gegessen). Lesson 1 builds the Perfekt with haben, the Partizip with ge-...-t and ge-...-en, and the Satzklammer around the rest of the sentence; lesson 2 adds sein for verbs of moving and changing (ich bin gelaufen, er ist eingeschlafen), sets spoken Perfekt beside written Präteritum, and ends with the skill check. Verbs whose helper differs by region (sitzen, stehen, liegen) are left out.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Perfekt mit haben | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Perfekt mit sein | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 17, fill_in_blank 11, matching 2, true_false 7, highlight_in_text 4, sorting 3. Builds on: `lit-de.verbs.simple-past`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-prefixes` teaches `lit-de.verbs.prefixes` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Eine Vorsilbe vor dem Verb macht ein neues Verb mit neuer Bedeutung: lesen, vorlesen; kaufen, verkaufen. Lesson 1 builds verbs with auf-, ab-, vor- and zer- and picks the one that fits a sentence; lesson 2 adds ver-, ent- and be-, says what the new verb means, shows that auf-, ab- and vor- move to the end of the sentence (Ich stehe auf.), and ends with the skill check.

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-present` teaches `lit-de.verbs.present` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The Präsens says what happens now or always. Lesson 1 recalls the endings (ich spiele, du spielst, er spielt) and names the Präsens; lesson 2 adds starke Verben whose vowel changes with du and er, sie, es (geben: du gibst, er gibt; fahren: du fährst, sie fährt; laufen: du läufst), while ich and wir keep the vowel, and ends with the skill check.

Verben sagen, was jemand tut oder was geschieht. Lesson 1 finds the verb in short sentences with the question "Was tut …?" and the ich-Probe (ich laufe, ich male); lesson 2 adds verbs for things that happen (es regnet, das Eis schmilzt), tells verbs from Nomen and Adjektiven, and ends with the skill check.

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-simple-past` teaches `lit-de.verbs.simple-past` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The Präteritum tells what happened, mostly in written stories. Lesson 1 forms it for schwache Verben with -te (spielen, er spielte; malen, wir malten); lesson 2 adds starke Verben that change their vowel and take no -te (gehen, ging; sehen, sah; kommen, kam; finden, fand), and ends with the skill check.

`de-DE.lit-de.verbs-tenses-mixed` teaches `lit-de.verbs.tenses-mixed` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The four Zeitformen side by side: Präsens, Präteritum, Perfekt and Futur. Lesson 1 names the Zeitform of a sentence by its verb parts (one word or two, werden or haben/sein); lesson 2 keeps one Zeitform through a short text, finds the verb that slips out of it and puts in the form that fits, and ends with the skill check.

## Oberbegriffe (Umbrella terms)

`de-DE.lit-de.word-categories` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.categories` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children sort words under an Oberbegriff and find words that belong to one. Lesson 1 works with pictures: sort them into Tiere, Obst and Kleidung, find the one that does not belong, and learn that a Möhre is Gemüse, not Obst. Lesson 2 works with written words: name the Oberbegriff for a group (Tisch, Stuhl, Bett: Möbel), find every word of one group in a list (Fahrzeuge, Schulsachen, Spielzeug), keep apart things that go together from things of the same kind (Schuh and Fuß), and ends with the skill check.

`de-DE.lit-de.word-fields` teaches `lit-de.vocabulary.word-fields` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

A Wortfeld holds words with a similar meaning. Lesson 1 collects the Wortfeld gehen (laufen, rennen, schleichen, hüpfen) and tells its words from words that are only about the same topic (Schuh, Weg); lesson 2 adds the Wortfeld sagen (rufen, flüstern, fragen, antworten), picks the word that fits a sentence best, and ends with the skill check.

`de-DE.lit-de.word-gaps` teaches `lit-de.sentences.word-gaps` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children learn where one word ends and the next begins. Lesson 1 counts the words in a spoken sentence, finds the gaps in a Wortschlange (Lenamaltgern) and spots a sentence with a gap in the wrong place. Lesson 2 builds sentences from muddled word cards (Satzsalat), using the capital letter at the start and the Punkt at the end, fills gap sentences with the word that makes sense, and ends with the skill check.

Taking in short phrases at a glance. Lesson 1 flashes a phrase for two seconds and the child picks what was written from phrases that differ in one small word; lesson 2 finds the word groups inside a sentence, reads a sentence in groups instead of word by word, and ends with the skill check.

Children read words exactly and tell apart words that differ in one letter: Hose and Rose, Maus and Haus, Tanne, Kanne and Tonne. Every task sets such look-alike words against each other, so a guess from the first letters or the length fails. Lesson 1 changes the first letter (Hose, Rose; Fisch, Tisch); lesson 2 changes a letter in the middle or the end (Tanne, Tonne; Bein, Boot, Brot) and ends with the skill check.

Children read and solve short word puzzles. Lesson 1 reads riddles of two short sentences ("Ich bin rund. Ich rolle.") and finds the picture that solves them; the voice never reads the riddle, so the child must. Lesson 2 plays Wortketten, where each word starts with the last letter of the word before (Ball, Lama, Apfel), and Buchstabensalat, where mixed-up letters are put back into a word, and ends with the skill check.

`de-DE.lit-de.word-stem` teaches `lit-de.spelling-strategies.word-stem` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Words that share a Wortstamm form a Wortfamilie, and the stem is spelt the same in all of them. Lesson 1 finds the stem (fahr in fahren, Fahrer, Fahrrad) and tells real family members from look-alikes (Fahne, Farbe); lesson 2 collects families with parts in front and behind (abfahren, Fahrt), across Nomen, Verben and Adjektive, and ends with the skill check.

Children write words and short sentences about pictures. Lesson 1 names what a picture shows with a word and its Begleiter (die Nase, der Igel): build the word from letter cards, pick its spelling, add the Begleiter. ## Schreiben, wie man spricht (Writing words by their sounds)

`de-DE.lit-de.write-by-sound` teaches `lit-de.spelling.write-by-sound` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children write words whose spelling follows their sounds (Oma, Nase, Limo, Tomate), using the letters they know. The strategy is the Mitsprechen: say the word slowly, one sound at a time, and write a letter for each sound. They build words from letter cards, fill the missing letter, count sounds and spot a spelling with a sound left out. Lesson 1 has short words of two open syllables; lesson 2 longer ones (Ente, Tomate, Esel) and ends with the skill check. Writing a whole word freely waits for a letter keyboard or handwriting recognition.

## Für wen und wozu schreibe ich? (Who am I writing for, and why?)

`de-DE.lit-de.writing-purpose` teaches `lit-de.writing.purpose` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Deciding the reader and the purpose before writing, and choosing the kind of text that fits. Lesson 1 names who a text is for and what it should do (einladen, informieren, erklären, unterhalten, sich bedanken); lesson 2 matches a writing task to its Textart and fits the words to the reader (du or Sie, the right greeting), and ends with the skill check. Writing the text itself waits for the write_text task type.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Leser und Zweck | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. Die passende Textart | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 25, ordering 1, matching 7, true_false 9. Builds on: `lit-de.writing.plan`, `lit-de.texts-media.text-types`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Plusaufgaben mit Zehnerübergang (Adding across ten)

`de-DE.math.add-bridge-10` teaches `math.add-sub-to-20.add-bridge-10` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 2.

Children add a one-digit number that crosses ten by filling up to ten first and adding the rest (8 + 5 = 8 + 2 + 3). Lesson 1 shows it on the Zwanzigerfeld, lesson 2 writes the split down and shows it on the Rechenstrich, lesson 3 uses it in short stories and ends with the skill check. Every lesson practises in three stages: full hints, one hint, no hints.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Erst bis zur 10 | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Zerlegen und weiterrechnen | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (quick) |
| 3. Rechengeschichten | no | 22 | warm-up, practise (5 full in context, 5 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 43, drag_and_drop_count 2, fill_in_blank 9, point_on_line 3, true_false 1, text_input 10. Builds on: `math.add-sub-to-20.missing-addend`, `math.numbers-to-20.partition-to-10`, `math.add-sub-to-20.analog-to-20`. Misconceptions addressed: 5.

## Schriftlich addieren mit Übertrag (Column addition with carrying)

`de-DE.math.add-carry` teaches `math.written-add-sub.add-carry` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

When a column makes ten or more, the ten moves to the next column as a small Übertrag. Lesson 1 carries from the Einer to the Zehner (347 + 238); lesson 2 carries in two columns and explains what the Übertrag means (ten Einer are one Zehner), and ends with the skill check.

## Flächeninhalt in Kästchen (Area in unit squares)

`de-DE.math.area-unit-squares` teaches `math.area-volume.area-unit-squares` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 finds the Flächeninhalt of figures on Karopapier by counting the Kästchen they cover, splits harder figures (L, T, U, a cross) into rectangles and adds the parts, and counts two half Kästchen as one. Lesson 2 finds the Flächeninhalt of a rectangle with a Malaufgabe (rows times Kästchen in a row, read from a filled rectangle or an outline), sees that different rectangles can have the same Flächeninhalt, splits figures into two rectangles, and ends with the skill check. Drawing a figure on the grid waits for a draw task.
