## Das Zweiersystem (Binary numbers)

`de-DE.math.binary` teaches `math.large-numbers.binary` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 sets the Zweiersystem beside our Zehnersystem: only the digits 0 and 1, and each place worth double the one to its right (Einser, Zweier, Vierer, Achter); it reads Dualzahlen up to four places as numbers, also as a row of lamps that are on (1) or off (0). Lesson 2 writes numbers to 31 in the Zweiersystem by taking the biggest place value that fits, writes a 0 for every place not needed, checks by working back, and ends with the skill check. Dualzahlen are spoken digit by digit (eins, null, eins) and never read as decimal numbers; options in binary are not read aloud.

|---|---|---|---|

Checking a result with the Umkehraufgabe and with an estimate (Überschlag), then finding and explaining mistakes in worked calculations. Lesson 1 checks minus with plus and estimates by rounding to tens; lesson 2 finds the forgotten ten, wrongly added ones, the swapped ones, a ten not taken away and the wrong operation, puts them right, and ends with the skill check.

## Im Kopf, halbschriftlich oder schriftlich? (Mental, informal or column method?)

`de-DE.math.choose-method` teaches `math.written-add-sub.choose-method` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Choosing how to calculate and saying why. Lesson 1 sorts tasks into im Kopf (round numbers, 8000 + 5000), halbschriftlich (a few steps, 6280 + 3450) and schriftlich (many digits, many Überträge); lesson 2 gives the reason for a choice, spots a clever shortcut (29 999 + 1), and ends with the skill check. More than one way can be right; the tasks ask for the way that suits best and say why in the feedback.

Clever ways within 1000 and choosing a way that suits. Lesson 1 uses a Hilfsaufgabe with a full hundred (458 + 199 = 458 + 200 − 1, 734 − 298 = 734 − 300 + 2) and puts the result right; lesson 2 changes both numbers of a minus sum by the same amount (gleichsinniges Verändern: 523 − 197 = 526 − 200), says which way suits which sum, and ends with the skill check.

## Geschickt rechnen (Calculating cleverly)

`de-DE.math.clever` teaches `math.add-sub-to-100.clever` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Two tricks for numbers just below a full ten. Lesson 1 recalls the Hilfsaufgabe for plus and carries it over to minus and to numbers two below a ten: add or take away the full ten, then put right what was too much (63 − 29 = 63 − 30 + 1, 58 + 28 = 58 + 30 − 2). Lesson 2 teaches gleichsinniges Verändern for minus: change both numbers the same way so the second becomes a full ten (52 − 18 = 54 − 20), and ends with the skill check.

Lesson 1 combines clothes and food systematically: take one T-Shirt and go through every Hose, then the next T-Shirt (rot → blau, grau; gelb → blau, grau: 4 Möglichkeiten), so nothing is missed, and sees that 2 T-Shirts and 3 Hosen make 3 + 3 = 6, not 5; lesson 2 lays two-digit numbers from Ziffernkarten in order (1 vorne, 2 vorne, 3 vorne), counts games when everyone plays everyone (Lea gegen Tim is the same game as Tim gegen Lea), and ends with the skill check. ## Chancen vergleichen (Comparing chances)

`de-DE.math.compare-chances` teaches `math.chance.compare-chances` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 compares chances by counting: the fields of a Glücksrad, the numbers on a Würfel that win (2, 4 und 6 sind gerade: 3 von 6), the two sides of a Münze; it also compares fields with the whole wheel (1 rotes von 2 Feldern beats 2 rote von 6 Feldern) and shows that a 6 is as likely as a 1. Lesson 2 makes a Vermutung and checks it with a Versuch in a Strichliste: which wheel fits a result, whether the result fits the guess, why a second Versuch comes out a little differently and why many Versuche tell more than a few. It ends with the skill check. Spinning or rolling in the app waits for an experiment task.

## Ist das Spiel fair? (Is the game fair?)

`de-DE.math.fair-games` teaches `math.chance.fair-games` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 13 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Zehn Zehner sind ein Hunderter | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Hunderter, Zehner und Einer | yes | 25 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 24, true_false 6, image_multiple_choice 6, text_input 8, fill_in_blank 4. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-100.place-value`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Umkehraufgaben und Aufgabenfamilien (Inverse sums and fact families)

`de-DE.math.inverse` teaches `math.add-sub-to-20.inverse` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Plus and minus undo each other: 3 + 5 = 8, so 8 − 5 = 3. Lesson 1 finds the Umkehraufgabe of a plus sum in the ten-frame and uses it to check a minus result; lesson 2 builds the whole family of four sums from three numbers (3 + 5, 5 + 3, 8 − 5, 8 − 3) and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Plus und minus gehören zusammen | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Aufgabenfamilien | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 19, text_input 1, fill_in_blank 16, true_false 10. Builds on: `math.add-sub-to-20.commutative`, `math.add-sub-to-20.sub-to-10`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Große Zahlen im Kopf (Large numbers in your head)

`de-DE.math.large-mental` teaches `math.add-sub-to-1000.large-mental` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Mental addition and subtraction with large round numbers, helped by a small sum. Lesson 1 adds and subtracts whole thousands (7000 + 5000, 15 000 − 8000) using 7 + 5 and 15 − 8; lesson 2 does the same with ten-thousands and hundred-thousands (250 000 − 50 000, 340 000 + 60 000), and ends with the skill check. Typed answers are written without the space; numbers shown on screen keep it.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|

## Mit Stufenzahlen malnehmen und teilen (Multiplying and dividing with round numbers)

`de-DE.math.large-mult-mental` teaches `math.mult-div-larger.large-mental` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Mental multiplication and division with round numbers, helped by a small times-table fact. Lesson 1 multiplies (3 · 400, 40 · 300) by using 3 · 4 and counting the zeros, including the trap 50 · 60 = 3000; lesson 2 divides (36 000 : 9, 2400 : 60) by using 36 : 9 and 240 : 6, and ends with the skill check. Typed answers are plain digits.

## Große Zahlen halbschriftlich (Informal written methods with large numbers)

`de-DE.math.large-semi-written` teaches `math.add-sub-to-1000.large-semi-written` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Adding and subtracting four-digit numbers halbschriftlich, with an Überschlag first. Lesson 1 rounds both numbers to thousands for the Überschlag and adds schrittweise (4600 + 2350: first the thousands, then the hundreds, then the tens); lesson 2 subtracts schrittweise, compares the result with the Überschlag to catch mistakes, and ends with the skill check. Four-digit numbers are written without a space (4600), on screen and in typed answers.

Adding and taking away ones across a ten, in steps via the full ten (38 + 5 = 38 + 2 + 3, 43 − 6 = 43 − 3 − 3). Lesson 1 adds: split the ones, jump to the next ten, then jump on, on the Rechenstrich. Lesson 2 takes away back to the full ten and on, and ends with the skill check.

## Eine Umfrage machen (Doing a survey)

`de-DE.math.plan-survey` teaches `math.data.plan-survey` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

The German number word says the ones first (vierundzwanzig), the digits write the tens first (24). Lesson 1 hears numbers and writes them, tens first; lesson 2 reads number words and digits and finds the swapped number, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Hören und schreiben | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Zahlwörter lesen | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 18, text_input 17, fill_in_blank 2, true_false 6, matching 3. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-100.tens`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

`de-DE.math.read-write-to-1000` teaches `math.numbers-to-1000.read-write` in de-DE, klasse-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

A three-digit number word in German says the hundreds first and then the two-digit part with its ones first (dreihundertachtundvierzig is 348). Lesson 1 hears numbers and writes them in digits; lesson 2 reads and writes number words, and ends with the skill check.

## Geteilt mit Rest (Division with remainders)

`de-DE.math.remainder` teaches `math.mult-div.remainder` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Divisions that do not come out exactly. Lesson 1 finds how many fit and what is left (17 : 5 = 3 Rest 2), checks with the Probe and learns that the Rest is smaller than the divisor; lesson 2 reads the Rest in a story (one more car for the children left over, or money left over), and ends with the skill check.

## Muster fortsetzen (Continuing patterns)

`de-DE.math.repeating-patterns` teaches `math.patterns.repeating` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 finds the part of a shape or colour pattern that repeats (Kreis, Dreieck, Kreis, Dreieck) and continues it; lesson 2 finds the gap in the middle of a pattern, the shape that breaks it and simple number patterns (1, 2, 1, 2), and ends with the skill check.

## Römische Zahlen (Roman numerals)

`de-DE.math.roman-numerals` teaches `math.large-numbers.roman-numerals` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 reads and writes römische Zahlzeichen to 20 with I, V and X: a smaller sign to the right of a bigger one adds, one to the left subtracts (IV, IX), and no sign stands more than three times in a row. Lesson 2 adds L and C, reads numbers to 100 in parts (tens, then ones), reads the hours on a clock face with Roman numerals, compares the Roman way with our Stellenwertsystem (an X is always ten, there is no zero) and ends with the skill check. The voice never reads a Roman numeral aloud, and Roman options are not read, so the child must read them.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. I, V und X | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none in context), check (quick) |
| 2. L und C: bis 100 | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 22, text_input 14, true_false 4, matching 2. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-1000.place-value`. Misconceptions addressed: 4.

## Vergrößern, verkleinern, Maßstab (Enlarging, reducing and scale)

`de-DE.math.scale` teaches `math.shapes.scale` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 enlarges and reduces rectangles and squares on Kästchenpapier by multiplying or dividing every side (2 und 3 Kästchen doppelt so groß: 4 und 6), shown as Punktefelder with one dot per Kästchen, against the mistake of adding the same number to every side; lesson 2 reads simple scales on plans (1 : 100, 1 cm sind 500 m), from plan to reality and back, and ends with the skill check. Drawing an enlarged figure on squared paper waits for a grid drawing task.

Subtracting a two-digit number from another in two ways. Lesson 1 takes away in steps on the Rechenstrich: first the tens, then the ones, across a ten when needed. Lesson 2 counts up from the smaller number (ergänzen), says when that is the quicker way, and ends with the skill check.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Abziehen in Schritten | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Ergänzen | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

## Anzahlen auf einen Blick (Seeing amounts at a glance)

`de-DE.math.subitize-to-10` teaches `math.numbers-to-20.subitize-to-10` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children learn to see how many without counting one by one. Lesson 1 uses dice patterns to 6, shown for two seconds so counting is not possible. Lesson 2 uses the Kraft der 5: rows of five and the ten-frame, where a full row is five and the rest is added on (seven is five and two). It ends with the skill check.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Würfelbilder | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Die Kraft der 5 | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

## Figuren symmetrisch ergänzen (Completing symmetrical figures)

`de-DE.math.symmetry-complete` teaches `math.symmetry.complete` in de-DE, klasse-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 mirrors one row of Kästchen at a senkrechte Spiegelachse, starting next to the axis, and tells mirroring from copying (■ □ □ │ □ □ ■, not ■ □ □ │ ■ □ □); lesson 2 completes and checks figures of two and three rows, mirrors to the left and across a waagerechte Achse, finds the row with a mistake, and ends with the skill check. The figures are rows of Kästchen in the task text, so the child fills each mirrored Kästchen from a chip. Colouring Kästchen on squared paper and drawing the mirror half of a picture wait for a drawing task.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Eine Reihe spiegeln | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Figuren ergänzen und prüfen | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: true_false 13, multiple_choice 13, fill_in_blank 16. Builds on: `math.symmetry.identify`, `math.shapes.draw-freehand-ruler`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Symmetrie um uns herum (Symmetry around us)

`de-DE.math.symmetry-environment` teaches `math.symmetry.in-environment` in de-DE, klasse-4. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 finds symmetry in nature and in things (Schmetterling, Tanne, Tisch von vorn; nicht die Tasse mit Henkel), in flags, and in reflections on water, where the Symmetrieachse is the waagerechtes Ufer; lesson 2 finds symmetry in words (OTTO und UHU senkrecht, KOCH und BOX waagerecht), Spiegelschrift on an ambulance, stars, snowflakes, Mandalas and Klecksbilder, and ends with the skill check. Taking photos of symmetry around the child or making a picture waits for a camera or drawing task.

`de-DE.math.symmetry-identify` teaches `math.symmetry.identify` in de-DE, klasse-1. Ages 6-7, about 10 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Lesson 1 folds in the mind: a figure is symmetrisch when both halves fit exactly on each other along the fold (Schmetterling, Herz, die Buchstaben A und T); lesson 2 says where the Symmetrieachse runs (senkrecht durch die Mitte, waagerecht), finds letters and shapes that are not symmetric, and ends with the skill check. Drawing the mirror half waits for a drawing task.

Dividing a three- or four-digit number by a one-digit number in writing, starting from the left. ## Stellenwert bis 1000 (Place value to 1000)

`en-GB.math.place-value-to-1000` teaches `math.numbers-to-1000.place-value` in en-GB, year-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Three-digit numbers in a place value chart (H, T, O) and the value of each digit: in 352 the 5 is worth 50. Lesson 1 puts numbers in the chart and partitions them; lesson 2 finds 10 or 100 more or less than a number, which Year 3 asks for, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. H, Z und E | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (quick) |
| 2. 10 und 100 mehr oder weniger | yes | 25 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 28, text_input 6, fill_in_blank 8, image_multiple_choice 2, true_false 4. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-1000.hundreds`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Zahlen bis 1000 lesen und schreiben (Reading and writing numbers to 1000)

`en-GB.math.read-write-to-1000` teaches `math.numbers-to-1000.read-write` in en-GB, year-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

English says three-digit numbers in digit order (three hundred and forty-eight is 348), so the trap is writing each part as heard (300408) or losing a zero (three hundred and four as 34). Lesson 1 hears numbers and writes them in digits; lesson 2 reads and writes number words, including the spelling of forty, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Hören und schreiben | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Zahlwörter bis 1000 | yes | 25 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 18, text_input 19, fill_in_blank 3, true_false 5, matching 3. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-1000.place-value`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Runden auf Zehner und Hunderter (Rounding to the nearest 10 and 100)

`en-GB.math.round-to-1000` teaches `math.numbers-to-1000.round` in en-GB, year-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Rounding finds the nearest multiple of 10 or 100, using the multiples either side from the last skill. Lesson 1 rounds to the nearest 10; lesson 2 rounds to the nearest 100, with the rule that halfway rounds up, and uses rounding to estimate a total in pounds. Ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Auf Zehner runden | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Auf Hunderter runden | yes | 25 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (skill) |

Task types: fill_in_blank 5, multiple_choice 27, text_input 11, true_false 5. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-1000.order-compare`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Zahlenfolgen bis 1000 (Counting in steps to 1000)

`en-GB.math.sequences-to-1000` teaches `math.numbers-to-1000.sequences` in en-GB, year-3. Ages 8-10, about 12 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Year 3 counts from 0 in multiples of 4, 8, 50 and 100, and in tens from any number across a hundred. Lesson 1 counts in 4s, 8s, 50s and 100s; lesson 2 finds the rule of a sequence, the missing term and the term that breaks the rule, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. In Vierer-, Achter- und Fünfzigerschritten | yes | 23 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Regeln finden | yes | 25 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 6 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 30, fill_in_blank 11, true_false 6, ordering 1. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-1000.order-compare`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Minus mit Zehnerübergang (Subtracting back through ten)

`en-GB.math.sub-bridge-10` teaches `math.add-sub-to-20.sub-bridge-10` in en-GB, year-1. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Subtracting across ten in two steps: back to 10 first, then the rest (13 − 5 = 13 − 3 − 2 = 8). Lesson 1 splits the number to take away and hops back to ten on a number line; lesson 2 checks answers with the inverse, practises all facts from 11 to 18, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Erst bis zur 10 | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Minus bis 20 | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: text_input 11, fill_in_blank 13, multiple_choice 11, true_false 7. Builds on: `math.add-sub-to-20.add-bridge-10`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Was bedeutet minus? (What does take away mean?)

`en-GB.math.sub-meaning` teaches `math.add-sub-to-20.sub-meaning` in en-GB, year-1. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Subtraction is taking away: some are eaten, fly off or go home. Lesson 1 finds the subtraction for a picture with crossed-out counters or a short story; lesson 2 matches stories to number sentences, writes them with − and =, and sets adding and taking away side by side, and ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Weg ist weg | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Plus oder minus? | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full in context, 6 some in context, 5 none in context), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 32, text_input 1, fill_in_blank 7, true_false 6. Builds on: `math.add-sub-to-20.add-meaning`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Minusaufgaben bis 10 (Subtracting to 10)

`en-GB.math.sub-to-10` teaches `math.add-sub-to-20.sub-to-10` in en-GB, year-1. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Subtracting within 10 by taking away in the ten frame and by counting back on the number line. Lesson 1 takes away and counts back within 5, with zero; lesson 2 works within 10, takes away a whole row of five at once, and ends with the skill check.

Task types: multiple_choice 16, point_on_line 8, text_input 16, true_false 6. Builds on: `math.add-sub-to-20.sub-meaning`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Anzahlen auf einen Blick (Seeing amounts at a glance)

`en-GB.math.subitize-to-10` teaches `math.numbers-to-20.subitize-to-10` in en-GB, reception. Ages 4-5, about 8 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Children see how many without counting one by one (subitising). Lesson 1 uses dice patterns to 5, shown for two seconds, as the EYFS goal asks. Lesson 2 goes on to "five and a bit": a full row of a ten frame is five, and seven is five and two. It ends with the skill check.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Würfelbilder | yes | 18 | learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Die Kraft der 5 | yes | 19 | learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 23, image_multiple_choice 8, true_false 6. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-20.count-to-5`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Die Zahlen von 11 bis 20 (Numbers to 20)

`en-GB.math.teens` teaches `math.numbers-to-20.teens` in en-GB, reception. Ages 4-5, about 8 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Counting beyond ten: a full ten frame is ten, and the numbers to 20 are ten and some more. Lesson 1 counts on from a full ten frame to say the number; lesson 2 makes teen numbers as ten and some more and finds them among other numbers, and ends with the skill check. Spoken questions; numbers say themselves.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Weiter als zehn | yes | 18 | learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Zehn und noch ein paar | yes | 19 | learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: multiple_choice 19, true_false 11, image_multiple_choice 7. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-20.partition-to-10`. Misconceptions addressed: 2.

## Sachaufgaben: Frage, Rechnung, Antwort (Word problems: question, calculation, answer)

`en-GB.math.word-problems-y2` teaches `math.word-problems.question-calc-answer` in en-GB, year-2. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Word problems with numbers to 100 in three steps: find the question, choose and do the calculation, and give the answer as a sentence. Lesson 1 decides between add and subtract and works out the answer; lesson 2 spots the number you do not need, picks the answer sentence that really answers the question, and ends with the skill check. Calculations add or subtract tens, or two-digit numbers with no crossing of ten, and the voice reads every story aloud.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Frage und Rechnung | yes | 20 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Wichtig und unwichtig | yes | 21 | warm-up, learn, practise (5 full, 5 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: text_input 17, multiple_choice 24. Builds on: `math.word-problems.picture-stories`, `math.add-sub-to-20.fluency-to-20`. Misconceptions addressed: 3.

## Die Ziffern 0 bis 10 (Numerals 0 to 10)

`en-GB.math.write-numerals-to-10` teaches `math.numbers-to-20.write-numerals-to-10` in en-GB, year-1. Ages 6-7, about 11 minutes a lesson. Status: draft, version 1.

Year 1 opens by reading and writing the numerals and matching each to its amount, as children move from Reception's spoken numbers to written ones. Lesson 1 takes 1 to 5, lesson 2 takes 6 to 10, each with how the numeral is formed; lesson 3 adds 0 and the number words one to ten (the National Curriculum asks for numerals and words in Year 1) and ends with the skill check. When a task asks which numeral fits, the numeral options have no speaker, because hearing "three" would give the answer.


| Lesson | New | Tasks | Activities |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Die Ziffern 1 bis 5 | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 2. Die Ziffern 6 bis 10 | yes | 22 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (quick) |
| 3. Null und Zahlwörter | yes | 24 | warm-up, learn, practise (6 full, 6 some, 5 none), check (skill) |

Task types: tap_to_count 1, multiple_choice 25, tracing 14, audio_choice 12, matching 7, true_false 9. Builds on: `math.numbers-to-20.count-to-10`. Misconceptions addressed: 4.
